2026-04-02T12:28:24.3156533Z   script: const projectId = 'iUe1Y4';
const project = 'native android';
const branch = context.payload.pull_request.head.ref;
const currentsUrl = `https://app.currents.dev/projects/${projectId}/runs?branch=${branch}&lookback=7`;
const linkLine = `🔍 **Suite ${project} test results:** [View in Currents](${currentsUrl})`;

// Markers for the shared section (written once)
const SECTION_START = '<!-- CURRENTS-LINKS:SECTION:START -->';
const SECTION_END   = '<!-- CURRENTS-LINKS:SECTION:END -->';
const sectionHeading = `## 🔍 Currents Test Results`;

// Markers for this specific project's link
const projectKey = project.replace(/\s+/g, '-').toUpperCase();
const LINK_START = `<!-- CURRENTS-LINK:${projectKey}:START -->`;
const LINK_END   = `<!-- CURRENTS-LINK:${projectKey}:END -->`;
const newLinkSection = `${LINK_START}\n${linkLine}\n${LINK_END}`;

const pr = await github.rest.pulls.get({
  owner: context.repo.owner,
  repo: context.repo.repo,
  pull_number: context.payload.pull_request.number,
});

let body = pr.data.body || '';

if (body.includes(LINK_START)) {
  console.log(`Currents link for project "${project}" already present. Skipping.`);
  return;
} else if (body.includes(SECTION_START) && body.includes(SECTION_END)) {
  // Section exists but this project's link does not yet — insert before SECTION_END.
  const insertAt = body.indexOf(SECTION_END);
  body = body.slice(0, insertAt) + newLinkSection + '\n' + body.slice(insertAt);
} else {
  // Neither section nor link exists — append a brand-new section.
  const sep = body.endsWith('\n\n') ? '' : body.endsWith('\n') ? '\n' : '\n\n';
  const newSection =
    `${SECTION_START}\n${sectionHeading}\n\n${newLinkSection}\n${SECTION_END}`;
  body = body + sep + newSection;
}

await github.rest.pulls.update({
  owner: context.repo.owner,
  repo: context.repo.repo,
  pull_number: context.payload.pull_request.number,
  body,
});
console.log(`Currents link for project "${project}" updated in PR description.`);
